Generated by `scripts/rust_core/evidence.py` from the gate run `234c3adff5f4de8c2348bac3ecc17cfc6f43241f.json` (commit `234c3adff5f4de8c2348bac3ecc17cfc6f43241f`), SHA-256 `45cb1c60f798614b2a4de85f2db912952d65fd8302453ec0ff65c9caa3e58e7b`, which stays outside the tree (design.md:432).
